Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The planetary operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 526. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 836 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to planetary structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 526/836 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
